- Empty-canvas drag in the Button Editor marquee-selects placements; plain selection replaces, while Shift, Ctrl, or Meta toggles every clicked or boxed Button into or out of the selection. Every selected placement has a crisp pointer-inert host outline that follows drag previews without extending a glow or badge beyond the actual rectangle. The sidebar Selected Buttons summary box is removed; selection outlines and multi-selection remain available in the workspace. Dragging any selected Button moves the full selection by one snapped, clamped, swept-collision-checked delta and one undo transaction without changing relative offsets, sizes, rows, gaps, order, or z-index; the Fan owner remains independently selectable and is excluded from content Re-order. `Align top-left Button to corner` rigidly changes only selected content `x/y` so its actual top-left Button reaches the surface corner while the Fan owner stays fixed. `Align to the Top Left button` keeps that anchor fixed and packs the same selected content rows and order to the current Button Sizing gap on both axes. Both fail atomically rather than moving the owner, unselected Buttons, or a Button to another row.
- Pop-out settings v1 remains backward-compatible: absent interaction/owner fields normalize to Pop with no owner. A Tool Set Pop-out settings file may be loaded into another installed Tool Set with the same child count; it maps children by declared slot and then saved order, preserves the target owner, actions, fields, labels, ownership, and activation behavior, and applies only saved layout/skin presentation and Pop/Fan behavior.
- `FlowCellFrontend/src/button/windows/`, `FlowCellFrontend/src/button/animations/`, `FlowCellFrontend/src/lib/windowContext.ts`, `FlowCellFrontend/src/lib/layoutSnapshots.ts`, and `FlowCellFrontend/src/AppBase.tsx` own Button editor, popout, fan, and transient activation-presenter window creation/routing/restore.
- Main owns FlowCell application shutdown. A native Main close request, including Windows taskbar `Close all windows`, first closes registered Editor, Pop, Fan, and installed Page windows through their cleanup-aware lifecycle, requests close for every remaining utility window, and destroys Main only after that secondary-window pass completes; a managed close failure leaves Main open and reports the failure instead of stranding an unknown partial shutdown.
- Illustrator-scoped popouts temporarily detach their native owner while Windows taskbar previews are active, retain the real Illustrator owner through that preview, and restore it afterward so activating another program immediately returns the popout behind Illustrator.
- Program-scoped Pop, Fan, and ordinary installed-page windows use one native placement state machine. An explicitly opened Pop/Fan receives a post-show normal-band reveal tied to the opening FlowCell window and then to the revealed window itself. Only an exact manifest `processNames` match for the actual foreground process may select `TOPMOST`; transparent Pop/Fan windows otherwise remain in the normal band with geometry-selective input even when the owning program is closed or inactive, preventing visible controls from passing clicks to unrelated windows underneath. Opaque installed pages retain normal input and may continue in the normal band when focused, while a Page with manifest `window.alwaysOnTop: true` bypasses the scoped state machine and remains native topmost. Installed pages use standard native chrome for dragging, minimizing, and closing. Taskbar previews still suppress input and demote every scoped window behind the real foreground HWND (or to the bottom as a fail-safe), while Illustrator's exact foreground match retains its owner-bound topmost behavior. Scoped-window IPC commands that share the native placement worker's apply lock execute off the main thread, preventing WebView focus callbacks from deadlocking against worker-dispatched Tauri window access.
- An explicit Pop/Fan open rehomes a saved semantic frame only when it has zero overlap with every connected monitor work area, preserving its size inside the monitor containing the opening FlowCell window. Passive `reveal: false` layout restore remains coordinate-verbatim.
- Layout restore and collapse/expand use saved physical-pixel managed-window bounds and canonical Button surface identities, preserving mixed-DPI size and owner anchors. Restore resolves its target program and panel before exposing either selection, then performs a request-scoped source refresh; whichever matching refresh wins also consumes the target-scoped saved Button selection, including an explicitly empty selection, instead of relying on unchanged React dependencies to reload cleared Buttons.
- Pop and Fan setups save a `surface`, `hitbox`, or `visual` window-fit mode. Their non-resizable transparent Tauri host is a large fixed canvas covering the active monitor work area (and any semantic frame outside it), starts cursor-ignored, and becomes cursor-active whenever the pointer is over a Button host, tool field, or Pop resize handle; after a Button receives pointer-down, the host stays cursor-active through pointer-up even if an authored animation moves the live core away. Owning-program state controls native topmost promotion, not whether a visible control can be clicked. Transparent gaps still pass clicks to the application underneath, and taskbar-preview suppression still disables the host. Hitbox/visual fits, hover/press/hold/play overflow, Fan expansion, and tool-set collapse/expansion move and resize only an absolutely positioned semantic content frame inside that canvas, preserving one invariant physical surface origin instead of repeatedly moving the HWND. The canvas grows or rehomes only when content would escape it, including monitor-edge drag/resize and mixed-DPI transitions. An unsized Pop starts at one design pixel per WebView CSS pixel; restored physical bounds use the destination WebView's effective pixel ratio, and scaled skin measurement retains skin-root and animated shadow/outline scale while normalizing ancestor content scale into design units. Hold Space and drag any visible Button to move the semantic frame; an expanded tool set stays expanded throughout that drag and persists the expanded placement. Pop corner handles track the visible Button/tool-field envelope even when that content is inset inside a larger saved surface, while resizing still scales and persists the complete semantic surface, preserves aspect, and anchors the opposite visible grip across mixed-DPI canvas changes. On release, real physical-pixel Pop/Fan bounds and semantic layout snapshots persist, while transient active envelopes and the large native canvas bounds never do.- One shared native input worker samples cursor and modifier state and emits only changed snapshots; each Pop/Fan reacts to those changes plus local geometry/DOM invalidation instead of polling native input every frame. Scope and input listeners subscribe before querying their initial state, retry transient setup/read failures, and fail closed to cursor-ignored; the serialized native cursor-style controller also retries a failed `setIgnoreCursorEvents` call and converges to the newest requested state. Newly created transparent windows initialize cursor-ignore fail-closed, while reopening an existing window leaves its live hitbox controller in sole ownership of that flag; Pop/Fan hitboxes start only after final canvas placement completes. Physical semantic bounds and cursor snapshots are mapped through the WebView's effective pixel ratio (including WebView zoom, with native DPI as fallback), so fixed-canvas placement and synthetic hover stay aligned. The window rejects pointers outside its semantic frame before consulting cached interactive membership, but reads exact viewport rectangles live after position-only frame movement, and it bypasses the render barrier for unchanged envelopes. Active visual overflow is measured only while the authored subtree has running motion plus final settle frames, so finite hover/play transitions stop consuming animation-frame work without changing skin source or hitbox ownership.
- Layout snapshots are strict current version 10 `FlowCellWindowLayout` documents and accept Button Editor, Pop-out, Fan, and installed Page windows with complete, per-kind, unique stable identities. Version 9 legacy layouts remain readable when they contain only the established window records. The native Save picker defaults to the dedicated `.flowlayout.json` filename but uses a single `.json` extension filter and normalizes an entered `.flowlayout` suffix, avoiding Windows appending that multipart suffix twice while preserving ordinary `.json` names. Load Layout applies no file-type filter, so every existing layout remains visible, while `.flowtheme.json` is rejected before any managed window is closed. A Main-selected settings-file Pop remains an ordinary Button Pop-out record, adding its file, choice, and panel owner solely to rebuild its noncanonical draft during restore. There is no compatibility parser or migration path for other versions or window kinds.
- The transient activation setup/runtime presenters are intentionally outside global layout snapshots: they exist only while configuring or playing, and the last physical bounds persist with the canonical Button assignment.
- The frameless Buttons Editor window renders its own title bar (all load/error/ready states): drag the bar to move, double-click it to maximize/restore, with minimize, maximize/restore, and close controls. Close routes through the window's close-requested handler so unsaved placement, skin, and animation drafts follow the normal cancellation path. Layout capture retains the Editor's last usable native bounds while minimized, and layout restore rejects Windows' `-32000` minimized-sentinel coordinates.
- Other FlowCell utility windows remain managed Tauri windows but do not own a separate Button implementation.
- Blender's action installer has an opt-in registry-only path for internal cross-program handoffs. It validates and copies one Python action into the bridge registry without creating a Blender panel record or canonical Button; ordinary Blender Button installs retain their existing owner and panel behavior.
- `FlowCellFrontend/src-tauri/capabilities/default.json` must list every managed or transient window label and every window/webview command the frontend calls, including the managed Add Program and Add Panel setup windows, the activation presenter, both background-color grants for transparent windows, and the force-destroy grant used only after Editor cleanup; a missing grant surfaces as a runtime "not allowed" ACL error, and changes to it require a Tauri rebuild.

Validation
----------
- Frontend/Tauri changes: run the relevant frontend build; use `flowcellbackend/helpers/Start-FlowCellFrontend.ps1` with `-ForceRestart` when the running frontend must refresh. A fresh helper launch arms a unique Button-bootstrap health marker and succeeds only after Main clears it; a fresh nonempty bootstrap error, early process exit, or bounded wait timeout fails the handoff instead of reporting a broken runtime as healthy.
- Rust changes: run `cargo check` plus focused tests.
- AutoHotkey changes: validate with AutoHotkey v2 and restart the hidden backend.
- PowerShell changes: parse-check touched scripts and run the narrow behavior test when practical.
- Blender source/bridge changes: compile-check Python where applicable and verify after add-on reload or Blender restart.
- Documentation-only changes: validate headings, links/paths, and stale-term removal; no application build is required.
- Portable program ZIPs contain only Git-tracked package files, require a tracked program manifest, and reject manifest-declared mutable folders, reparse points, containment escapes, and unsafe archive members; the Core ZIP contains no `Programs/` payload.

Update Rules For This File
--------------------------
- Update this file whenever FlowCell ownership, runtime paths, canonical state, or major architecture changes.
- Keep it concise: one contract or owner per bullet, with focused docs carrying the details.
- Do not add historical compatibility narratives, incident timelines, or per-feature implementation diaries.

- Save Blender Parent folder and Existing project Browse dialogs share a persisted `lastBrowseFolder` in owner-state and reopen at that directory, falling back to the configured parent folder for older state. Cancel preserves the last directory and project destination. The generic `folder.select` capability forwards an optional `initialDirectory` to the native picker.
- An installed Local package and its active `Panels/.../*.flowcell-source.json` owner record must have identical `bundledSourceId` and `bundledSourceVersion`, alongside matching owner/program/panel identity. A script-only hotfix must preserve this metadata pair; version upgrades use the normal package synchronization transaction. Execution rereads both records, so repairing their consistency does not require restarting FlowCell.

- XYZ Dimensions 1.0.3 connects each requested axis result to its existing child Button label through hidden display fields and the shared fieldPatch/labelField contract. X/Y/Z initially show -- in and update on activation, including clearing an axis when no object is selected. Geometry, units, skins, placements, owner/child IDs, and click-only querying stay unchanged; normal catalog version synchronization imports the readout fields and behavior into existing owners.

- Illustrator Lithophane 1.0.3 fixes native imageCapture silently producing no PNG when the owned runtime path plus original image name exceeded the Windows legacy path limit. Captures now use compact unique request filenames; deeper runtime paths use a persistent FlowCell/lithophane-exports cache under Illustrator user application data so Blender's saved image references remain valid. Existing files are not overwritten. Physical dimensions, sublayer naming, alpha capture, source documents and selections remain unchanged. Native captures passed in the exact installed 225-character runtime folder and a forced 363-character fallback case; the long-name behavioral regression failed before the fix and all 22 focused cases passed afterward. Normal catalog version resync updates the installed Illustrator owner; final installed button handoff follows separately.
